=== idx: 25 ===
Gold supporting: detailed mechanistic analysis of gevokizumab, an allosteric anti - il - 1β antibody with differential receptor - modulating properties.
LLM supporting:  gevokizumab targets interleukin - 1β ( il - 1β ) by negatively modulating its signaling. specifically, it decreases the binding affinity of il - 1β for the il - 1 receptor type i ( il - 1ri ), which is the primary signaling receptor for il - 1β. this inhibition occurs through an allosteric mechanism, where gevokizumab alters the electrostatic surface potential of il - 1β, thereby reducing the association rate between il - 1β and il - 1ri. as a result, the recruitment of the il - 1 accessory protein, necessary for full activation of the il - 1ri, is also impaired. importantly, gevokizumab does not affect the il - 1 receptor type ii ( il - 1rii ), a decoy receptor that regulates the availability of il - 1β. by specifically targeting the il - 1β / il - 1ri interaction, gevokizumab effectively inhibits the proinflammatory effects of il - 1β while leaving the il - 1β pathway available for host defense responses.

Gold supporting: angiosperms synthesize s - methylmethionine ( smm ) from methionine ( met ) and s - adenosylmethionine ( adomet ) in a unique reaction catalyzed by met s - methyltransferase ( mmt ).
LLM supporting:  the s - adenosylmethionine ( smm ) cycle, which involves the interconversion of smm and homocysteine, is a key metabolic pathway in angiosperms. smm serves as a methyl donor for the synthesis of methionine ( met ) from homocysteine, catalyzed by the enzyme homocysteine s - methyltransferase ( hmt ). this cycle helps to prevent the depletion of free met and the accumulation of smm, which can be toxic to the cell. the smm cycle is essential for maintaining the balance of smm and met, and it operates in various tissues of the plant, including leaves, roots, and developing seeds. the primary function of the ssm cycle is to stop the free met pool from being depleted by an overshoot in adomet synthesis, rather than to prevent the depletion of free met itself. this is supported by the in silico model of the smm cycle in mature arabidopsis leaves, which indicates that the cycle consumes half the adomet produced and suggests that the cycle serves to stop accumulation of adomet. therefore, the s - adenosylmethionine ( smm ) is the methyl donor in this cycle, and it is used to maintain the metabolic balance of met and smm in the plant.
